# NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) Mathematics - Continuity and Differentiability

## Topic 2: Differentiability

**Total Questions:** 50

---

### Q1. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable columnspacing="1.4ex" columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mi>p</mi></msup><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>p</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>0</mn><mo>&lt;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mi>p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mi>p</mi></msup><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>

Since, <math><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, therefore it is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>p</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mrow></math>

As <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> exists finitely

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> exists finitely

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></math> exists finitely

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>p</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>p</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi></math>

</details>

### Q2. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider a function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>,</mo></math> where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>≥</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> If <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous as well as differentiable for all <math><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

For <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be continuous and differentiable <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> must be continuous and differentiable <math><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi></math>
Since, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is continuous for all <math><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo></math> therefore, it is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> also.
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> … <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Also, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Hence, <math><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo></math>

</details>

### Q3. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then the number of points where <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is non-differentiable is/are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>7</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>6</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Using the graph of <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/237b3a12-d956-4a70-bb20-f58b4f73f197.png" style="width:2.27986in;height:1.55347in" />
Clearly, from the graph,
we can see that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is non-differentiable at <math><mn>6</mn></math> points

</details>

---

### Q4. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo> </mo><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** discontinuous at exactly two points
- **(B)** differentiable <math><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi></math>
- **(C)** differentiable <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** continuous <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>-</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="{" close="}"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) differentiable <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
Continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
 <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
For differentiability,
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"> <mrow> <mtable columnalign="left" equalcolumns="true" equalrows="true"> <mtr columnalign="left"> <mtd columnalign="left"> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mtd> <mtd columnalign="left"> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr columnalign="left"> <mtd columnalign="left"> <mn>0</mn> </mtd> <mtd columnalign="left"> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr columnalign="left"> <mtd columnalign="left"> <mn>1</mn> </mtd> <mtd columnalign="left"> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">'</mi></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo></math>non-differentiable
at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">'</mi></msup><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">'</mi></msup><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo></math>non-differentiable</p>

</details>

### Q5. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous and differentiable at any point, then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>, </mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>, </mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>, </mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>, </mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>, </mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The given function is clearly continuous at all points except possibly at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>±</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo></math>
For <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo></math> we must have
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>α</mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> … <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Now, for <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo></math> we must have
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>α</mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> <math><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>∵</mo><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>∴</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>α</mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>α</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Putting <math><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> in <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> we get <math><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

### Q6. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If f(x) = <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>λ</mi><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mn>0</mn><mo>≤</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>μ</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>12,</mn><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&lt;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>9</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>μ</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **8**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>L</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>L</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>R</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>L</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math>

<math><mi>&#955;</mi><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn></math> <math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>+4</mo></math> ......(1)

Also <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> <math><mi>L</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>D</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>R</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>D</mi></math>

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#183;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#956;</mi></math> ......(2)

By (1) and (2)</p><p><math><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q7. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> (where, <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> denotes the greatest integer function). Then, <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mo>′</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** does not exist
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) does not exist**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
Now, LHL of <math> <mrow>  <msup>   <mrow>    <mfenced>     <mrow>      <mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mo>&#x2032;</mo>  </msup>  <mfenced>   <mrow>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow>  </mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder>   <mrow>    <mi>lim</mi></mrow>   <mrow>    <mi>h</mi><mo>&#x2192;</mo><msup>     <mn>0</mn>     <mo>+</mo>    </msup>    </mrow>  </munder>  <mfrac>   <mrow>    <mfenced>     <mrow>      <mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow>    </mfenced><mfenced>     <mrow>      <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow>    </mfenced><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mfenced>     <mrow>      <mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow>    </mfenced><mfenced>     <mrow>      <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mrow>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>→</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">∞</mi></math>
<math> <mrow>  <mo>&#x2234;</mo><msup>   <mrow>    <mfenced>     <mrow>      <mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mo>&#x2032;</mo>  </msup>  <mfenced>   <mrow>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow>  </mfenced></mrow></math> does not exist.

</details>

### Q8. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>max</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>≤</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>12</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&lt;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>≤</mo><mn>12</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> If <math><mi>S</mi></math> is the set of points in the interval <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> at which <math><mi>f</mi></math> is not differentiable, then <math><mi>S</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** equal to <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** equal to <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** an empty set
- **(D)** equal to <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) equal to <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Here, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>12</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>12</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>12</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/c0237cb5-44dc-4cf5-a8f5-3a7c13ce3248.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:2.97361in;height:1.37986in" />
Hence, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>S</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q9. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>, then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>g</mi><mi> </mi></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>g</mi></math>  is not differentiable at  <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

In the neighborhood of <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo></math> <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo></math> <math><msup><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>′</mi></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>–</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo></math> <math><msup><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>′</mi></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q10. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>f</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mtext>x</mtext></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left" columnspacing="1.4ex"><mtr><mtd><msup><mtext>x</mtext><mrow><mtext>p</mtext><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mtext>x</mtext></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mtext>:x</mtext><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mtext>:x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>,</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> then at <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> the function <math><mtext>f</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>x</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** Continuous if <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and differentiable if <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** Continuous if <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and differentiable if <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** Continuous and differentiable if <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) Continuous if <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and differentiable if <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Continuity at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math>
<math><mtext mathvariant="normal">L</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">H</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">L</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal"></mtext><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">l</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">m</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mtext mathvariant="normal">f</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">l</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">m</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">cos</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>i</mtext><mtext>f&#160;p</mtext><mo>&#62;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mtext mathvariant="normal">R</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">H</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">L</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal"></mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal"></mtext><munder><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">l</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">m</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mtext mathvariant="normal">f</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">l</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">m</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">cos</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>i</mtext><mtext>f&#160;p</mtext><mo>&#62;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
and <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">f</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">f</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">x</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0&#160;</mn><mtext mathvariant="normal"></mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">f&#160;</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal"></mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext><mo>&#62;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Differentiability at <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>:
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>L.H.D.</mtext><mo>=</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mtext>h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mtext>f</mtext><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>h</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>f</mtext><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">l</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">m</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">cos</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">l</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">m</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">cos</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> if <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>R.H.D.</mtext><mo>=</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mtext>h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mtext>h</mtext><mrow><mtext>p</mtext><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mtext>h</mtext></mfrac><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mtext>h</mtext></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">l</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">m</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><msup><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">cos</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> if <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">f</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">x</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> if <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal"></mtext><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q11. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8901;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>y</mi></mfenced></math> for all <math><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>y</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math> and <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>&#981;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math>. If <math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#981;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>, then <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced></mrow> </math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><msup><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder> <mrow> <mi>lim</mi></mrow> <mrow> <mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> </munder> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow> </mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced></mrow> <mi>h</mi> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8901;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>h</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8901;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>y</mi></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi><mi>&#981;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>h</mi></mfenced><mi>log</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>&#981;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mi>log</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#981;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>log</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>&#8757;</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#981;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>h</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></math>

</details>

---

### Q12. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The number of points at which the function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0.5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is not differentiable in the interval <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math> is/are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mn>3</mn> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math> <mn>3</mn> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The curve will have sharp corner at <math><mi>x</mi><mi> </mi><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mn>0.5</mn></math> and at <math><mi>x</mi><mi> </mi><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn></math> and a point of discontinuity at <math> <mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mi>π</mi> <mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>.
Hence, total number of points where the function is not differentiable is <math> <mn>3.</mn> </math>

</details>

---

### Q13. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt><mo>,</mo><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mo>∀</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3,3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the number of point(s) where <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is non-differentiable is/are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Using graph of
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math> ,
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
In the figure, we can see the graph of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/de973fde-33e2-423b-b976-9655f956811e.png" style="width:2.85347in;height:1.44028in" />
Clearly, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is non-differentiable at
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn></math> ( <math><mn>4</mn></math> points)
because there are sharp points at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> and at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>3</mn></math> there are vertical tangents.

</details>

### Q14. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>f</mi><mi></mi><mo>:</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>→</mo><mi>R</mi></math> be a function defined by <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">min</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>.</mo></math> If <math><mi>K</mi></math> be the set of all points at which <math><mi>f</mi></math> is not differentiable, then <math><mi>K</mi></math> has exactly

**Options:**

- **(A)** one element
- **(B)** two elements
- **(C)** five elements
- **(D)** three elements

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) five elements**

**Detailed Solution:**

Graph of given function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">min</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo></math> is as shown
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/516e41da-f42d-4a07-a390-32d09645ea0d.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.92014in;height:1.50694in" />
Clearly, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not differentiable at <math><mn>5</mn></math> points in <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>.

</details>

### Q15. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"><mtable columnalign="left" columnspacing="1.4ex"><mtr><mtd><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>. At <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mtext>g</mtext></math> is differentiable but <math> <msup>  <mtext>g</mtext>  <mo>&#x2032;</mo> </msup> </math> is not continuous
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> is differentiable while <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></math> is not differentiable
- **(C)** both <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> are non differentiable
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> is differentiable and <math> <msup> <mtext>g</mtext> <mo>′</mo> </msup> </math> is continuous

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> is differentiable and <math> <msup> <mtext>g</mtext> <mo>′</mo> </msup> </math> is continuous**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>We have, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable columnspacing="1.4ex" columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
For <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>g</mtext> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mtext>x</mtext> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mi>cos</mi><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mtext>x</mtext> </mfrac> <mfenced> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mi>sin</mi><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mtext>x</mtext> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>
For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mtext>g</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mtext>x</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mtext>g</mtext><mfenced><mtext>x</mtext></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>g</mtext><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mtext>x</mtext><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mtext>x</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mtext>x</mtext><mn>3</mn></msup><mi>sin</mi><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfrac><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mtext>x</mtext></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mtext>x</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><msup><mtext>g</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mtext>x</mtext></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable equalrows="true" equalcolumns="true"><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><msup><mtext>x</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>sin</mi><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mtext>x</mtext></mfrac><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>cos</mi><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mtext>x</mtext></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math> <msup> <mtext>g</mtext> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> </math> is continuous at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Also, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> is differentiable at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> .</p>

</details>

### Q16. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>f</mi><mo>:</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>→</mo><mi>R</mi></math> be a function defined by <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">max</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></mrow></mrow></math> If <math><mi>K</mi></math> be the set of all points at which <math><mi>f</mi></math> is not differentiable, then <math><mi>K</mi></math> has exactly

**Options:**

- **(A)** two elements
- **(B)** one element
- **(C)** three elements
- **(D)** five elements

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) three elements**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/dunkgpjjdhepqgkhyvfflzzpbczipvoljvyqlbjqnplunpyxwx.png" />

From the graph it can be concluded that <math><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is non-differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext>  </mtext><mo>±</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> in <math><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>

i.e., at <math><mn>3</mn></math> points in <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>
