### Q17. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≥</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1,</mn></math> then <math><mn>4</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></math> can be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>π</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mi>π</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Also <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> must be continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi></mi><mi></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"> <mrow> <mtable equalcolumns="true" equalrows="true"> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow> <mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>;</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow> <mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>;</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
Now for <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> or <math><mn>0</mn><mi></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
From equation <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, we get
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#177;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> or <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, <math><mn>4</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>&#960;</mi></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q18. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>. Then, in the interval <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>g</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** not differentiable at one point
- **(B)** differentiable at all points
- **(C)** not continuous
- **(D)** not differentiable at two points

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) not differentiable at one point**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
And <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2,2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>g</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"> <mrow> <mtable equalcolumns="true" equalrows="true"> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>0</mn> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mn>0</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><msqrt> <mn>2</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow> <mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <msqrt> <mn>2</mn> </msqrt> <mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mspace linebreak="newline"/><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>&#8722;</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
So, <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>

</details>

---

### Q19. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo></math> where [·] and {·} represent the greatest integer function and the fractional part function respectively, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>3</mn><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q20. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the incorrect statement is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> both are continuous <math><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous <math><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math>
Now, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8746;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mn>1,2</mn></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8746;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mn>1,2</mn></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable columnalign="left" equalrows="true" equalcolumns="true"><mtr><mtd><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mo>&#8734;</mo><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8746;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8734;</mo></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd/></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd/></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
Clearly, <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>2</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mtext>'</mtext><mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced></mrow> </math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
and <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced></mrow> </math> is non-differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q21. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Given <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>{</mo><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mtext>    0</mtext><mo>≤</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>sgn</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mtext>    </mtext><mn>1</mn><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></math>

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></math> is differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1,</mn></math> then the value of <math> <mrow> <mrow><mo>|</mo> <mrow> <mi>a</mi><mo>−</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow> <mo>|</mo></mrow></mrow> </math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></math>

For continuity at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi>i</mi></mfenced></math>

In <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

In <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></math>

For differentiability at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> . This with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>)</mo></math> gives the values <math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>−</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

</details>

### Q22. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi></math>, then <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(C)** non differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** non differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) non differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>&#8734;</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8746;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#8734;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mn>1,5</mn></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> both are continuous<math><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> is also continuous <math><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math>
Now,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable equalrows="true" equalcolumns="true"><mtr><mtd><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mo>&#8734;</mo><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8746;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8734;</mo></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
Now, check differentiability
At <math> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></math>
<math><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>3</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>3</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> </math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>at</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>differentiable</mi></math> 
At <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>5</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>125</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>10</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>5</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>125</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>10</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> </math>
Clearly, <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>5</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>5</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> non-differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q23. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable everywhere, then <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mn>9</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn></math> &hellip; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Now, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is also differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></math>
<math><mn>6</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn></math> &hellip; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
From <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> &amp; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>16</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q24. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><msup><mn>2</mn><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mn>2</mn><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** continuous and differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** continuous but not differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** differentiable but not continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) None of these**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>For function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
L.H.L. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mn>2</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mn>2</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
R.H.L. <math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math><math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Since, <math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math></p><p>therefore, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not continuous and not differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q25. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">∞</mi></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous and differentiable for all <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>.</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous but not differentiable for all <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>.</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at infinite number of points.
- **(D)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at two points.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous and differentiable for all <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>.</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>.</mo></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Clearly, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math>
Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is continuous and differentiable <math><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q26. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The number of points where <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is non-differentiable is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>3</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Graph of <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/5e355ba6-35f1-4a6e-8987-9b31d4215acb.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:2.4in;height:1.34653in" />
From the graph, we see that <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is non-differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>4</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q27. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>−</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>−</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>−</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>−</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>−</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>−</mo><mfenced><mi>b</mi></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>−</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>−</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup><mo>−</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mfenced><mtext>i</mtext></mfenced></math>
For this limit to exist, it must be of <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> form.
<math><mo>∴</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
From <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mtext>i</mtext></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∵</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>−</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q28. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder> <mrow> <mtext>lim</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup> <mn>0</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </munder> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>0</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow> </mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mn>0</mn> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>&#8658;</mo><munder> <mrow> <mtext>lim</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup> <mn>0</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </munder> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow> </mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfenced> <mi>b</mi> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math>

For limit to exists, it must be <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> form

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> ... <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

From <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> ... <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q29. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mrow><mtable columnspacing="1.4ex" columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>sin</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>,&#160;</mo></mrow></mfenced></math>then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is continuous and differentiable at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous but non differentiable at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous but differentiable at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous and non differentiable at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous but non differentiable at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let us shift the graph of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> in the direction of the negative <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo></math>axis by <math><mn>4</mn></math> units
&amp; let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> will have the same behaviour as of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>.</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac bevelled="true"><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfrac></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
Continuity at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>,
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>.</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac bevelled="true"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>.</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac bevelled="true"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
Differentiability at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mtext>h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mtext>g</mtext><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>h</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>g</mtext><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>h</mtext></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mtext>h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mtext>sin</mtext><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>h</mtext></mrow></mfenced><msup><mtext>tan</mtext><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac bevelled="true"><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mtext>h</mtext></mfrac></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi>n&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac bevelled="true"><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac bevelled="true"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mtext>h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mtext>g</mtext><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mtext>h</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>g</mtext><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced></mrow><mtext>h</mtext></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mtext>h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mtext>sin&#160;h</mtext><mo>.</mo><msup><mtext>tan</mtext><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac bevelled="true"><mn>1</mn><mtext>h</mtext></mfrac><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mtext>h</mtext></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi>n&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac bevelled="true"><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac bevelled="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced></math>
Clearly, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is non-differentiable at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is non-differentiable at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

</details>

### Q30. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<div><p align="left" style="text-align:left;"><span>On differentiating  <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mi>tan</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msqrt> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </msqrt> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mi>x</mi> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow> </mrow> </math> with respect to </span><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math><span>, the result would be</span></p></div>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <div><p align="left" style="text-align:left;"><span style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <mo>.</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math> </span></p></div>
- **(B)** <div><p align="left" style="text-align:left;" ><span  style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math>  <mrow>   <mfrac>    <mn>1</mn>    <mrow>     <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup>      <mi>x</mi>      <mn>2</mn>     </msup>         </mrow>   </mfrac>     </mrow> </math>  </span></p></div>
- **(C)** <div><p align="left" style="text-align:left;" ><span  style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math>  <mrow>   <mfrac>    <mn>2</mn>    <mrow>     <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup>      <mi>x</mi>      <mn>2</mn>     </msup>         </mrow>   </mfrac>     </mrow> </math>  </span></p></div>
- **(D)** <div><p align="left" style="text-align:left;" ><span  style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math>  <mrow>   <mfrac>    <mn>1</mn>    <mn>2</mn>   </mfrac>   <mo>.</mo><mfrac>    <mn>1</mn>    <mrow>     <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi>    </mrow>   </mfrac>     </mrow> </math>  </span></p></div>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <div><p align="left" style="text-align:left;"><span style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <mo>.</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math> </span></p></div>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<div><p align="left" style="text-align:left;"><span>Let us assume that,</span></p><p align="left" style="text-align:left;"><span style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math> <mrow> <mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup> <mrow> <mi>tan</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msqrt> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </msqrt> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mi>x</mi> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow> </mrow> </math> </span></p><p align="left" style="text-align:left;"><span>Put :  <math> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>tan</mi><mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </math> </span></p><p align="left" style="text-align:left;"><span style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi>x</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>tan</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>θ</mi></msqrt><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>tan</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>sec</mi><mi>θ</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>tan</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfrac><mi>θ</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mfrac><mi>θ</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>cos</mi><mfrac><mi>θ</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>=</mo><mi>tan</mi><mfrac><mi>θ</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr></mtable></math> </span></p><p> </p><p><span>On differentiating with respect to <i>x</i>:</span></p><p align="left" style="text-align:left;"><span style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math> <mtable columnalign="left"> <mtr> <mtd> <mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup> <mi>tan</mi> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mi>tan</mi><mfrac> <mi>θ</mi> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <msup> <mi>tan</mi> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mi>x</mi> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mo>⇒</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <mo>.</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>.</mo> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable> </math> </span></p></div>

</details>

### Q31. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi></math>, then the set of all values of <math><mi>x</mi></math> at which <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is not differentiable is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mn>5,10</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>10,15</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mn>5,10,15</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇒</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Points where <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is non-differentiable are
<math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> &amp; <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
<math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> &amp; <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>10</mn></math>

</details>
