### Q32. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

<p>If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>p</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable everywhere,</p><p>then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **5.85**

**Detailed Solution:**

Continuity at<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>p</mi></math>
Continuity at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>9</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>3</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi></mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> <math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q33. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For a differentiable function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math>, if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mn>3</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mn>3</mn></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>

Given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mn>3</mn></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mn>3</mn></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mn>2</mn> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mn>3</mn> ### Q34. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>. If <math><mi>&#955;</mi></math> is the number of points at which <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is non-differentiable, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **43.20**

**Detailed Solution:**

Using graph of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#38;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>, we get, 
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is non differentiable at
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/53a46a1d-aadc-4c4b-9db1-948444bee15e.png" style="width:3.11389in;height:1.63333in" />
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>5</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>
Hence, <math><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>

</details>

### Q35. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable columnspacing="1.4ex" columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>p</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mi>q</mi></msqrt></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is differentiable everywhere, then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>71</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>51</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>33</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>71</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> Continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mi>q</mi></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><msqrt><mi>q</mi></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mfenced></math>
Continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>9</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>3</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced><mn>3</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi>ii</mi></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>p</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mtable><mtr/><mtr/></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> Differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi>iii</mi></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> Differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>3</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mn>3</mn></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>6</mn><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi>iv</mi></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math> {from equations <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>}
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>p</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>q</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>a</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>b</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>71</mn><mn>10</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q36. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>f</mi><mi></mi><mo>:</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>R</mi></math> be a function such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>y</mi></mfenced></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>′</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mn>0</mn> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo></mrow> </math> then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is a quadratic function
- **(B)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous but not differentiable
- **(C)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable in <math><mi>R</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is bounded in <math><mi>R</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable in <math><mi>R</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

We have, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mn>0</mn> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder> <mrow> <mi>lim</mi></mrow> <mrow> <mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> </munder> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow> </mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced></mrow> <mi>h</mi> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><munder> <mrow> <mtext>lim</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> </munder> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>3</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow> </mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mn>0</mn> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mn>0</mn> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow> </math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi></math>

</details>

### Q37. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math> <mrow> <mtext>f</mtext><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext>g</mtext></mrow> </math> and <math> <mtext>h</mtext> </math> are differentiable functions. If <math> <mrow> <mtext>f</mtext><mfenced> <mn>0</mn> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>;</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext>g</mtext><mfenced> <mn>0</mn> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>;</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext>h</mtext><mfenced> <mn>0</mn> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow> </math> and the derivatives of their pair wise products at <math> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> </math> are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mtext>fg</mtext></mfenced><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mtext>;</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup><mfenced><mtext>gh</mtext></mfenced><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mtext>hf</mtext></mfenced><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mtext>fgh</mtext></mfenced><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **16**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>y</mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext>fgh</mtext></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mtext>dy</mtext><mtext>dx</mtext></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mtext>f</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup><mtext>gh</mtext><mo>+</mo><msup><mtext>fg</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup><mtext>h</mtext><mo>+</mo><msup><mtext>fgh</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup></math>

        <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mtext>f</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup><mtext>gh</mtext><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mtext>fg</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup><mtext>h</mtext><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mtext>fgh</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

        <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mtext>h</mtext><mfenced><mrow><msup><mtext>f</mtext><mo mathvariant="bold">'</mo></msup><mtext>g</mtext><mo>+</mo><msup><mtext>g</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup><mtext>f</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mtext>g</mtext><mfenced><mrow><msup><mtext>f</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup><mtext>h</mtext><mo>+</mo><msup><mtext>fh</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mtext>f</mtext><mfenced><mrow><msup><mtext>g</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup><mtext>h</mtext><mo>+</mo><msup><mtext>gh</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>

        <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mtext>h</mtext><mtext>.</mtext><msup><mfenced><mtext>fg</mtext></mfenced><mo>'</mo></msup><mo>+</mo><mtext>g</mtext><mtext>.</mtext><msup><mfenced><mtext>fh</mtext></mfenced><mo>'</mo></msup><mo>+</mo><mtext>f</mtext><mtext>.</mtext><msup><mfenced><mtext>gh</mtext></mfenced><mo>'</mo></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

       <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mtext>fgh</mtext></mfenced><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mtext>h</mtext><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced><msup><mfenced><mtext>fg</mtext></mfenced><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mtext>g</mtext><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced><msup><mfenced><mtext>fh</mtext></mfenced><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mtext>f</mtext><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced><msup><mfenced><mtext>gh</mtext></mfenced><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>

                     <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>6</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>5</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>8</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>6</mn> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q38. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left" columnspacing="1.4ex"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>,</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> then <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** Continuous as well as differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** Continuous but not differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** Differentiable but not continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) None of these**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>⁡</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>⁡</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>⁡</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>⁡</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>⁡</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Since, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>⁡</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≠</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>⁡</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> therefore, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not continuous and not differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math>

</details>

### Q39. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Which of the following is a correct statement?

**Options:**

- **(A)** Continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math> is sufficient for differentiability at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
- **(B)** Differentiability at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math> is sufficient for continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
- **(C)** Existence of limit at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math> is sufficient for continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
- **(D)** Differentiability at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math> is necessary for existence of tangent at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) Differentiability at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math> is sufficient for continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>, then <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is also continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
Hence, differentiability at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math> is sufficient for continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q40. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">max</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>n</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>N</mi></math> has maximum points of non-differentiability for <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math> cannot be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">max</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/31d18787-2eb7-486f-a896-eb92849d4e88-image4.png" style="height: 150px; width: 350px;" />
Thus, for the maximum points of non-differentiability, graphs of <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> and <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math> must intersect at maximum number of points which occurs when <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>3.5</mn></math>

</details>

### Q41. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>p</mi><mi>cos</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>cos</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>r</mi><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is continuous in <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the number of points at which <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not differentiable is/are

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

At <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>p</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
At <math><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
At <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>r</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>N</mi><mi>D</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>N</mi><mi>D</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q42. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">min</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>, then the number of points where <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is non-differentiable is/are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>7</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>6</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Using the graph of <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/89f0cc93-5e41-4e0d-815a-213259637b90.png" style="width:1.96042in;height:1.40694in" />
Clearly, from the graph we can see <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is non-differentiable at <math><mn>6</mn></math> points.

</details>

### Q43. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></math> upto <math><mi>∞,</mi></math> then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math> does not exist
- **(B)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous but not differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math> (as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
So, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q44. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><msqrt><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>≤</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≥</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable <math><mo>∀</mo><mi></mi><mi>x</mi><mo>≥</mo><mn>0,</mn></math> then <math><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>240</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>85</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>250</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>5</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> must be continuous and differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
For continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi>a</mi><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
For differentiability at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"> <mrow> <mtable equalcolumns="true" equalrows="true"> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow> <mfrac> <mi>a</mi> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><msqrt> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow> </msqrt> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>;</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mn>0</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mi>b</mi> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>;</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>2</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>b</mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
From <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>18</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>

</details>

### Q45. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≥</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Since, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is differentiable,  it must be continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> ... <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable columnalign="left" equalrows="true" equalcolumns="true"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>

Now, for <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>

<math><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> ... <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

From equation <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> &amp; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q46. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>, then (where, [.] represents the greatest integer function)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
and <math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
Since, <math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≠</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> therefore, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math>

</details>

### Q47. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><msqrt><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>≤</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>9</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≥</mo><mn>9</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is differentiable for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>≥</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math><mn>5</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>b</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>240</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>80</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>250</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>10</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> must be continuous and differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
For continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>9</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></math> &hellip;. <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
For differentiability at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></msup><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"><mtable columnalign="left" equalcolumns="true" equalrows="true"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mi>a</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>9</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>9</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>9</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>9</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>b</mi></math> &hellip; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
From <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>32</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>23</mn></mfrac></math>
Hence, <math><mn>5</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>40</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>46</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q48. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><msqrt><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>≤</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≥</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable <math><mo>∀</mo><mi></mi><mi>x</mi><mo>≥</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then <math><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>9</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> must be continuous and differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
For continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi>a</mi><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mi></mi><mi></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
For differentiability at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"> <mrow> <mtable equalcolumns="true" equalrows="true"> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow> <mfrac> <mi>a</mi> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><msqrt> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow> </msqrt> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>;</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mn>0</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mi>b</mi> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>;</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>2</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>b</mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
From <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>)</mo></math>, we get, </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>18</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>16</mn></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q49. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>min</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt><mo>,</mo><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mrow></mfenced><mo> </mo><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the number of points where <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is non-differentiable is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Clearly, from the graph we can see that <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is non-differentiable at <math><mn>4</mn></math> points

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/22_djad.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 250px; height: 172px;" />

</details>

### Q50. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

In <math> <mrow> <mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow></mrow></math>, the total number of points where <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>.</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>–</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not differentiable, are equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(B)** 4
- **(C)** 5
- **(D)** 6

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>3</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The graph of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>.</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>–</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/562154956.JPG" style="width: 250px; height: 200px;" />

<math> <mo>⇒</mo> </math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not differentiable at <math> <mrow> <mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mi>π</mi> <mn>4</mn> </mfrac> <mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mi>π</mi> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi> </mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>.
 
<math><mo>⇒</mo> </math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not differentiable at <math><mn>3</mn></math> points

</details>
